/**
 * ── タイトル・テキスト整形ヘルパー ──
 */
export function sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(text: string): string {
  if (!text) return '';
  return text.replace(/[殺死血狂暴]/g, '★').replace(/(死ね|殺す|血まみれ|狂気|虐殺)/g, '危険').trim();
}
export function cleanEpisodeTitle(title: string): string {
  if (!title) return '';
  return title
    .replace(/^[\s🎵🎬💡🏮🎨⚓🖼️✨🔥\uD800-\uDBFF\uDC00-\uDFFF\u2600-\u27BF]+/g, '')
    .replace(/^【.*?】\s*/, '')
    .replace(/^(第\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+\s*[話曲章幕弾節巻]|Episode\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+|Ep\.\s*\d+|Track\s*\d+|Vol\.\s*\d+|Part\s*\d+|Chapter\s*\d+)[:：\s・\-ー]*/i, '')
    .replace(/(第\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+\s*[話曲章幕弾節巻]|Episode\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+|Ep\.\s*\d+|Track\s*\d+|Vol\.\s*\d+|Part\s*\d+|Chapter\s*\d+)[:：\s・\-ー]*/gi, '')
    .replace(/[\(（【\[]\s*(第\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+\s*[話曲章幕弾節巻]|Episode\s*\d+|Vol\.\d+|Track\s*\d+)\s*[\)）】\]]/gi, '')
    .replace(/[\(（【\[]\s*[起承転結・\s]+\s*[\)）】\]]/gi, '')
    .replace(/^[「『](.*?)[」』]$/, '$1')
    .trim();
}
export function cleanEpisodeTitleEn(title: string): string {
  if (!title) return '';
  return title
    .replace(/^(Episode\s*[\d０-９一二三四五六七八九十百千]+|Ep\.\s*\d+|Track\s*\d+|Vol\.\s*\d+|Part\s*\d+|Chapter\s*\d+)[:：\s・\-ー]*/i, '')
    .replace(/[\(（【\[]\s*(Episode\s*\d+|Vol\.\d+|Track\s*\d+)\s*[\)）】\]]\s*$/i, '')
    .trim();
}
export function checkIsHistorical(era: string = '', theme: string = ''): boolean {
  const combined = `${era} ${theme}`;
  if (['現代', '令和', '平成', 'バブル', 'SNS', 'ブラック企業', '社内不倫', 'タワマン', '推し活', 'マッチングアプリ'].some(k => combined.includes(k))) {
    return false;
  }
  return ['江戸', '幕末', '明治', '大正', '戦後', '昭和', '戦国', '平安', '鎌倉', '室町', '安土桃山', '古代', '中世', '大河', '吉原', '新選組', '浪人', '大名', '武士', '侍', '寺子屋', '飛脚', '岡っ引き', '火消し', '屋台めし', '薬売り', '鉄火場', '鼠小僧'].some(k => combined.includes(k));
}
export function extractHighlights(narrationText: string, suggestedWords: string[] = []): Array<{ word: string; color: string; sizeScale: number }> {
  if (!narrationText || !narrationText.trim()) return [];
  const validHighlights: Array<{ word: string; color: string; sizeScale: number }> = [];
  const neonColors = ['#FFE600', '#00F0FF', '#FF2A85', '#39FF14', '#FF7A00', '#BD00FF'];
  for (const rawWord of suggestedWords) {
    const word = (rawWord || '').trim();
    if (word && narrationText.includes(word) && word.length >= 1 && word.length <= 8 && !validHighlights.some(h => h.word === word)) {
      validHighlights.push({ word, color: neonColors[validHighlights.length % neonColors.length], sizeScale: 1.15 });
    }
  }
  const tryMatch = (pattern: RegExp, minLen = 2, maxLen = 6) => {
    if (validHighlights.length >= 2) return;
    const matches = narrationText.match(pattern);
    if (!matches) return;
    for (const word of Array.from(new Set(matches)).filter(w => w.length >= minLen && w.length <= maxLen)) {
      if (validHighlights.length >= 2) break;
      if (!validHighlights.some(h => h.word === word)) {
        validHighlights.push({ word, color: neonColors[validHighlights.length % neonColors.length], sizeScale: 1.15 });
      }
    }
  };
  tryMatch(/[\u4e00-\u9faf]{1,3}の[\u4e00-\u9faf]{1,3}/g, 3, 7);
  tryMatch(/[\u4e00-\u9faf]{2,4}/g, 2, 4);
  tryMatch(/[\u30a1-\u30f6]{2,6}/g, 2, 6);
  return validHighlights;
}
/**
 * 主人公キャラクターの三面図マスターシート生成プロンプトを構築
 */
export function buildCharacterTurnaroundPrompt(
  theme: string,
  country: string,
  era?: string,
  taste?: string,
  characterDna?: string,
  characterId?: string
): string {
  const fixedChar = (characterId && characterId !== 'auto')
    ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === characterId)
    : (characterDna ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => characterDna.includes(c.dnaPromptEn.slice(0, 24))) : undefined);
  const rawStyle = taste ? resolveTastePrompt(taste) : '';
  const isHist = checkIsHistorical(era, theme);
  let cleanStyle = rawStyle;
  if (!isHist && cleanStyle) {
    cleanStyle = cleanStyle.replace(/\b(edo period|feudal japan|ancient japan|ancient japanese)\b/gi, '').replace(/,\s*,/g, ',').trim();
  }
  if (fixedChar) {
    return `${fixedChar.turnaroundPromptEn}
Protagonist DNA: ${fixedChar.dnaPromptEn}.
${cleanStyle ? `Art style: ${cleanStyle}.` : ''}
CRITICAL MANDATE: EXACTLY THREE VIEWS (front, side, back) of ONE SINGLE INDIVIDUAL (${fixedChar.id}) aligned on a single clean white reference sheet. Maintain signature hair color, accessories, and outfit 100% faithfully.`;
  }
  const hasExplicitMale = /\b(male|boy|man|guy|men|masculine|男子|男の子|少年|男性|青年|オッサン|おじさん)\b/i.test(`${characterDna || ''} ${theme || ''}`);
  const defaultProtagonistDesc = hasExplicitMale 
    ? `Handsome aesthetic anime youth protagonist with neat modern styling.`
    : `Charming elegant anime female protagonist with neat modern school uniform attire.`;
  const protagonistProfile = characterDna ? `Protagonist design: ${characterDna}.` : defaultProtagonistDesc;
  const safeThemeDesc = (theme || 'Japanese youth drama').replace(/(少し着崩した|着崩した|ほどけかけた|乱れた|はだけた)/g, 'stylish').replace(/[^\w\s,\.\-]/g, ' ').trim() || 'Japanese school youth';
  return `Master character model sheet, three-view turnaround:
front view, side profile view, back view of the same single protagonist standing in a neutral pose side-by-side.
Full body from head to toe, perfectly consistent face, hairstyle, facial features, and attire.
${protagonistProfile} (Setting inspired by: ${safeThemeDesc})
Clean plain pure white background, professional animation concept art, character turnaround sheet, model sheet, masterpiece, highly detailed, 8k resolution.
${cleanStyle ? `Art style: ${cleanStyle}.` : ''}
CRITICAL MANDATE: EXACTLY THREE VIEWS (front, side, back) of ONE SINGLE INDIVIDUAL aligned on a single sheet. Absolutely NO multiple different people, NO cluttered props, NO extra poses!`;
}
/**
 * シリーズ全体のグランドデザイン（最大20話チャンクリレー対応プロンプト）
 */
export function buildGrandDesignChunkPrompt(
  startEp: number,
  endEp: number,
  totalCount: number,
  country: string, 
  theme: string, 
  era?: string, 
  isMangaMode?: boolean, 
  isMvMode?: boolean,
  characterDna?: string,
  characterName?: string,
  previousEpisodesHeaders?: string
): string {
  const worldSetting = era && era !== theme ? `${theme} (時代: ${era}, 地域: ${country})` : `${theme} (${country})`;
  const countInChunk = endEp - startEp + 1;
  const isFirstChunk = startEp === 1;
  const contextClause = previousEpisodesHeaders
    ? `\nPREVIOUS GENERATED EPISODES (Episodes 1 to ${startEp - 1}):\n${previousEpisodesHeaders}\nCRITICAL: Continue seamlessly as subsequent standalone serial episodes ${startEp} to ${endEp}. Each episode must be high-integrity and individually compelling even if watched out of order.`
    : '';
  const protagonistInstruction = characterDna
    ? `CENTRAL PROTAGONIST: "${characterName || '主人公'}" (${characterDna}). Maintain consistent protagonist presence.`
    : `Design engaging standalone and serialized story arc.`;
  return `Create episodes ${startEp} to ${endEp} (total planned series: ${totalCount} episodes) with Theme: "${worldSetting}".
${protagonistInstruction}
${isMangaMode ? "Design pacing for dynamic comic/manga serialization with dramatic hooks." : ""}
${isMvMode ? "Design poetic aesthetic musical visuals." : ""}
${contextClause}
CRITICAL RULES:
1. Generate EXACTLY ${countInChunk} episode plans from epNumber ${startEp} to ${endEp}.
2. "titleJp" and "titleEn" MUST BE PURE STANDALONE SUBTITLES (do NOT include "第1話" or "Episode 1").
3. Each episode must stand on its own without breaking continuity if viewed independently.
Output ONLY valid JSON:
{
  ${isFirstChunk ? `"seriesTitle": "${theme.split('（')[0]} Series",\n  "overallSynopsis": "Overview of entire narrative arc",` : ''}
  "episodesPlan": [
    { "epNumber": ${startEp}, "titleJp": "独自のサブタイトル", "titleEn": "Standalone English Subtitle", "summary": "この話のあらすじ（日本語2〜3行）" }
  ]
}`;
}
export function buildGrandDesignPrompt(
  count: number, 
  country: string, 
  theme: string, 
  era?: string, 
  isMangaMode?: boolean, 
  isMvMode?: boolean,
  characterDna?: string,
  characterName?: string
): string {
  return buildGrandDesignChunkPrompt(1, Math.min(20, count), count, country, theme, era, isMangaMode, isMvMode, characterDna, characterName);
}
/**
 * 続編エピソード（第N話）のプロットを自律策定するためのプロンプト
 */
export function buildNextEpisodePlanPrompt(
  nextEpId: number,
  seriesTitle: string,
  overallSynopsis: string,
  previousEpisodes: Array<{ epNumber: number; titleJp: string; summary?: string }>,
  country: string,
  theme: string,
  era?: string,
  isMangaMode?: boolean
): string {
  const worldSetting = era && era !== theme ? `${theme} (時代: ${era}, 地域: ${country})` : `${theme} (${country})`;
  const prevSummary = previousEpisodes.map(p => `第${p.epNumber}話「${cleanEpisodeTitle(p.titleJp)}」: ${p.summary || ''}`).join('\n');
  return `You are a world-class drama director and screenwriter continuing serialized drama "${seriesTitle}".
World Setting: "${worldSetting}". Overall Synopsis: "${overallSynopsis}".
Previous Narrative History:
${prevSummary}
Create compelling story outline for NEXT episode (Episode ${nextEpId}).
${isMangaMode ? "Design with intense comic/manga cliffhangers and pacing." : ""}
CRITICAL: "titleJp" and "titleEn" must be standalone subtitles WITHOUT episode numbers (NEVER write "第${nextEpId}話").
Output ONLY valid JSON:
{
  "epNumber": ${nextEpId},
  "titleJp": "この話独自の具体的サブタイトル（第X話は入れない）",
  "titleEn": "Standalone English Subtitle without numbers",
  "summary": "この話のあらすじ・展開（日本語2〜3行）"
}`;
}
/**
 * 最大20カットずつの「チャンク・リレー生成」プロンプト
 */
export function buildCompactScriptChunkPrompt(
  epId: number, 
  currentPlan: SeriesEpisodePlan, 
  startCut: number,
  endCut: number,
  totalCutCount: number,
  country: string, 
  theme: string, 
  era?: string, 
  isMangaMode?: boolean,
  isMvMode?: boolean,
  taste?: string,
  _productionMode?: string,
  existingTitles?: string[],
  characterId?: string,
  previousCutsSummary?: string
): string {
  const worldSetting = era && era !== theme ? `${theme} (時代: ${era}, 地域: ${country})` : `${theme} (${country})`;
  const fixedChar = (characterId && characterId !== 'auto') ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === characterId) : undefined;
  const charDirective = fixedChar ? `\nPROTAGONIST LOCK: Protagonist MUST be "${fixedChar.nameJa}" (${fixedChar.dnaPromptEn}). Narration style: ${fixedChar.personality}. SAFETY: Never depict cruel abuse.` : '';
  const defaultCharDna = fixedChar ? fixedChar.dnaPromptEn : 'Consistent single protagonist matching the world setting and narrative';
  const rawStyle = taste ? resolveTastePrompt(taste) : '';
  const plannedTitleJp = cleanEpisodeTitle(currentPlan.titleJp || `場面${epId}`);
  const plannedTitleEn = cleanEpisodeTitleEn(currentPlan.titleEn || `Scene ${epId}`);
  const avoidDup = (existingTitles && existingTitles.length > 0) ? `AVOID DUPLICATE THEMES: Do NOT repeat [${existingTitles.join(', ')}].` : '';
  const isFirstChunk = startCut === 1;
  const isFinalChunk = endCut >= totalCutCount;
  const chunkCount = endCut - startCut + 1;
  const pacingDirective = !isFinalChunk
    ? `\n【重要・物語の進行に関する絶対指示】現在は全${totalCutCount}カット中の第${startCut}〜${endCut}カット目（物語の途中・展開中）です。ここで絶対に物語を完結させず、次のカット（Cut ${endCut + 1}以降）へ続く展開のままバトンを渡してください。事件の完全解決や総括の結びに入らず、次へ続く緊張感や新たな事態の発生を描いてください。結末（オチ・解決）は最終カット（Cut ${totalCutCount}）でのみ描かれます。`
    : `\n【最終バッチ指示】現在は全${totalCutCount}カットのクライマックスおよび結末部（Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut}）です。これまでの展開を受け止め、第${totalCutCount}カット目で見事な結末・オチ・余韻を描いてストーリーを美しく完結させてください。`;
  const relayHistoryClause = previousCutsSummary
    ? `\n--- PREVIOUS CUTS CONTEXT (Cut 1 to ${startCut - 1}) ---\n${previousCutsSummary}\nCRITICAL: Continue the story chronologically from Cut ${endCut < totalCutCount ? startCut : startCut}. Do not restart or repeat previous scenes.`
    : '';
  return `You are an elite film director, manga artist, and screenwriter.
Generate screenplay for Cut ${startCut} to Cut ${endCut} (Total Cuts planned: ${totalCutCount}) for Episode ${epId} in "${worldSetting}".
Episode Concept: "${currentPlan.summary || plannedTitleJp}".
Art Style: "${taste || ''}" (${rawStyle}).
${avoidDup}
${charDirective}
${pacingDirective}
${relayHistoryClause}
MANDATORY RULES:
1. Generate EXACTLY ${chunkCount} cuts starting from id: ${startCut} up to id: ${endCut}.
2. Ensure continuous story flow and visual variety across shot scales (Close-up, Medium, Wide).
3. Cut with "isObjectOnly": true should occasionally focus on an atmospheric symbolic keepsake or deserted prop without humans.
4. "basicPlot" MUST be clear, high-detail English description for text-to-image prompt.
5. "narrationJp" MUST be natural Japanese dialogue or poetic narration (around 20-40 characters).
6. Format: Output cuts as an array under the "cuts" field, even if there is only 1 cut.
Output ONLY valid JSON:
{
  ${isFirstChunk ? `"titleJp": "${plannedTitleJp}",
  "titleEn": "${plannedTitleEn}",
  "summary": "${currentPlan.summary || plannedTitleJp}",
  "characterDna": "${defaultCharDna}",
  "eraAnalysisJp": "時代背景・世界観の解説（日本語）",` : ''}
  "cuts": [
    { 
      "id": ${startCut}, 
      "isObjectOnly": false,
      "basicPlot": "Cinematic visual scene description of protagonist...", 
      "narrationJp": "セリフまたはナレーション（日本語）"
    }
  ]
}`;
}
export interface InfographicCoverPromptOptions {
  titleJp: string;
  titleEn?: string;
  coverCatchphraseJp?: string;
  coverCatchphraseEn?: string;
  theme?: string;
  era?: string;
  taste?: string;
  country?: string;
  productionMode?: string;
  characterDna?: string;
  styleDna?: string;
  forbiddenAnachronisms?: string[];
  authenticAttireEn?: string;
  isHorizontal?: boolean;
}
/**
 * 世界観・時代・ジャンルに完全適応するインフォグラフィック扉絵・16:9看板プロンプトを構築
 */
export function buildAdaptiveInfographicCoverPrompt(options: InfographicCoverPromptOptions): {
  promptEn: string;
  negativePromptEn: string;
} {
  const {
    titleJp,
    titleEn: _titleEn,
    coverCatchphraseJp,
    theme = '',
    era = '',
    taste = '',
    productionMode = 'episodes',
    characterDna,
    styleDna,
    forbiddenAnachronisms = [],
    authenticAttireEn = '',
    isHorizontal = false
  } = options;
  const isHist = checkIsHistorical(era, theme);
  const combinedContext = `${era} ${theme} ${productionMode}`.toLowerCase();
  const cleanTitle = cleanEpisodeTitle(titleJp) || titleJp;
  let cleanSubtitle = cleanEpisodeTitle(coverCatchphraseJp || '');
  if (cleanSubtitle === cleanTitle || cleanSubtitle.length < 2) cleanSubtitle = '';
  const formatDesc = isHorizontal
    ? `Cinematic horizontal 16:9 widescreen YouTube master thumbnail poster and editorial promotional billboard.`
    : `Vertical 9:16 high-impact editorial cover poster and commercial infographic key visual.`;
  let mediumInstruction = isHorizontal
    ? `Premium cinematic 16:9 master title card, high-CTR YouTube thumbnail visual, beautiful atmospheric lighting, elegant scenic composition`
    : `Premium cinematic vertical teaser poster, high-CTR commercial infographic design, striking visual hierarchy, professional cinematic color grading`;
  let typographyStyle = isHorizontal
    ? `Striking high-contrast Japanese title typography boldly displaying "${cleanTitle}" artistically embedded into a beautiful floating antique signboard or architectural title plaque in the upper or upper-side area with balanced breathing margin`
    : `Striking high-contrast Japanese poster headline typography reading "${cleanTitle}" centered inside floating title plaque in upper portion with safe breathing margin above`;
  let extraNegative = `cheap low-res flyer, amateur layout, chaotic clutter`;
  if (isHist) {
    mediumInstruction = isHorizontal
      ? `Authentic traditional Japanese panoramic key visual (modern Ukiyo-e and Nihonga-inspired illustration), expansive landscape with rich textured details, subtle washi texture`
      : `Authentic traditional Japanese artistic poster and editorial key visual (modern Ukiyo-e and Nihonga-inspired illustration), full-bleed scenic atmosphere with rich textured details, subtle washi texture, decorative border`;
    typographyStyle = `Prominent bold sumi-ink Japanese calligraphy plaque positioned in upper portion, displaying exact title "${cleanTitle}" with seal stamp`;
    extraNegative = `modern typography, digital fonts, English text overlay, plastic, neon cyberpunk, sneakers, modern clothing, wristwatch, smartphone`;
  } else if (productionMode === 'manzai' || combinedContext.includes('漫才') || combinedContext.includes('お笑い')) {
    mediumInstruction = `High-energy theatrical entertainment flyer poster (Yose comedy hall style), vibrant comedic composition, warm paper lanterns, bold stage lighting`;
    typographyStyle = `Dynamic Japanese theatrical headline typography boldly showing "${cleanTitle}" centered in floating marquee`;
    extraNegative = `dark horror, grim, somber, overly melancholic`;
  } else if (productionMode === 'trivia' || combinedContext.includes('雑学') || combinedContext.includes('解説')) {
    mediumInstruction = `Stunning commercial infographic editorial poster, high-CTR visual encyclopedia diagram layout, structured visual hierarchy, magazine cover aesthetic`;
    typographyStyle = `Bold high-impact Japanese editorial headline typography featuring "${cleanTitle}" centered in floating banner`;
    extraNegative = `messy collage, cluttered unintelligible scribbles, distorted diagrams`;
  } else if (productionMode === 'mv' || combinedContext.includes('音楽') || combinedContext.includes('mv')) {
    mediumInstruction = isHorizontal
      ? `Iconic widescreen music single master artwork and YouTube cover, evocative color grading, cinematic emotional narrative`
      : `Iconic music concept art poster and vinyl single cover design, evocative color grading, cinematic emotional narrative, subtle audio HUD visualizer accents`;
    typographyStyle = `Stylized Japanese music single headline typography reading "${cleanTitle}" centered in floating neon-accented audio plaque`;
    extraNegative = `boring layout, flat corporate diagram, low-effort snapshot`;
  }
  let focalSubject = characterDna 
    ? (isHorizontal 
        ? `Main focal subject: ${characterDna} ${isHist && authenticAttireEn ? `dressed in authentic ${authenticAttireEn}` : ''}, captured in cinematic medium shot positioned harmoniously alongside the title signboard in wide 16:9 view.`
        : `Main focal subject: ${characterDna} ${isHist && authenticAttireEn ? `dressed in authentic ${authenticAttireEn}` : ''}, captured in medium-wide shot (framed from waist up or seated low) in lower two-thirds of canvas below floating title plaque.`)
    : `Key thematic visual: Symbolic central subject embodying essence of "${theme}".`;
  const subtitleClause = cleanSubtitle ? `Integrated subtitle accent: "${cleanSubtitle}". (Do NOT write episode number digits).` : '';
  const resolvedTaste = taste ? resolveTastePrompt(taste) : (styleDna || '');
  const styleClause = resolvedTaste ? `Artistic rendering style: ${resolvedTaste}.` : '';
  const ratioClause = isHorizontal
    ? `Composition: Horizontal 16:9 widescreen aspect ratio, harmonious cinematic hierarchy, bright beautiful atmospheric lighting, masterpiece, 8k resolution.`
    : `Composition: Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio, harmonious editorial hierarchy. Main character framed below title plaque with ample breathing room. Masterpiece, 8k resolution.`;
  const promptEn = [
    formatDesc,
    mediumInstruction + `.`,
    `Poster Layout: Cohesive full-bleed scene. Dedicated title signboard boldly displaying ONLY exact title "${cleanTitle}". Mandatory: NEVER write episode numbers (NO "第1話", "Episode 1", "Vol.1")!`,
    focalSubject,
    typographyStyle + `.`,
    subtitleClause,
    styleClause,
    ratioClause
  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  const baseNegative = `episode number, episode counter, "第1話", "第2話", "Episode 1", "Episode 2", "Vol.1", numbered badge, giant blank void, banner touching extreme top edge, text crammed against ceiling, text overlapping character head, blurry, low resolution, bad anatomy, duplicate character, ugly, artifacts, dark heavy black bar, black border, dark navy tint`;
  const negativePromptEn = [baseNegative, extraNegative, forbiddenAnachronisms.join(', ')].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
  return { promptEn, negativePromptEn };
}
export function buildAntiPreviousCompositionNegative(
  prevScale?: string,
  prevAngle?: string,
  prevPrompt?: string
): string {
  return buildDynamicAntiPreviousNegative({
    scale: prevScale,
    angle: prevAngle,
    prompt: prevPrompt,
  });
}
export const buildImagePromptAndNegative = buildFinalCinematicPromptAndNegative;
/**
 * カットごとの演出データ（構図、テロップ、ケンバーン効果）を決定
 */
export async function directShot(
  task: GenerationTask,
  settings: GeneratorSettings,
  _activeReference: any,
  previousShotInfo?: PreviousShotContext
): Promise<Partial<Cut>> {
  const { cutId, prompt } = task;
  const preset = getStoryboardPreset(cutId, settings.isMvMode, settings.isMangaMode);
  const recCw = resolveRecommendedCameraWorkAndKenBurns(
    cutId,
    settings.productionMode,
    settings.isMvMode,
    settings.isMangaMode
  );
  const kbPreset: KenBurnsPreset = recCw.recommendedKenBurns;
  const isAllowedEyeContact = settings.isMvMode && isMvChorusCut(cutId, 30);
  const isHistorical = checkIsHistorical(settings.era, settings.theme);
  const defaultTelop = resolveRecommendedTelopStaging(
    cutId, 
    settings.isMvMode, 
    isHistorical, 
    previousShotInfo?.telop,
    settings.productionMode
  );
  const effectiveScale = task.shotScale || preset.scale;
  const effectiveAngle = task.cinematicAngle || preset.angle;
  let antiPreviousNegative = buildDynamicAntiPreviousNegative(previousShotInfo, effectiveScale);
  if (settings.isMvMode && !isAllowedEyeContact) {
    antiPreviousNegative = antiPreviousNegative ? `${antiPreviousNegative}, ${MV_ANTI_CAMERA_LOOK_NEGATIVE}` : MV_ANTI_CAMERA_LOOK_NEGATIVE;
  }
  const hasCameraDirective = prompt.includes('[CAMERA DIRECTIVE:') || prompt.includes('[STRICT') || prompt.includes('[IRONCLAD');
  const finalPromptEn = hasCameraDirective ? prompt : `${effectiveAngle}. ${prompt}`;
  return {
    promptEn: finalPromptEn,
    negativePrompt: antiPreviousNegative,
    cameraWork: task.camera || recCw.id,
    cameraMotion: recCw.motionPrompt,
    cinematicAngle: effectiveAngle,
    shotScale: effectiveScale,
    kenBurnsPreset: kbPreset,
    telop: {
      fullText: '',
      style: defaultTelop.style,
      transition: defaultTelop.transition,
      position: defaultTelop.position,
      directorNote: defaultTelop.directorNote
    }
  };
}
export interface GenerateSafeScriptOptions {
  epId: number;
  currentPlan: {
    epNumber?: number;
    titleJp: string;
    titleEn: string;
    summary?: string;
  };
  country: string;
  theme: string;
  era?: string;
  isMangaMode?: boolean;
  isMvMode?: boolean;
  taste?: string;
  productionMode?: string;
  existingTitles?: string[];
  characterId?: string;
  targetCutCount?: number;
  superBackoff?: boolean;
  abortCheck?: () => boolean;
  addLog?: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'process') => void;
}
export interface SafeScriptResult {
  coverCatchphraseEn?: string;
  titleJp: string;
  titleEn: string;
  summary: string;
  characterDna?: string;
  eraAnalysisJp: string;
  forbiddenAnachronisms: string[];
  authenticAttireEn: string;
  forbiddenKeywordsEn: string;
  coverCatchphraseJp: string;
  highlightWords: string[];
  cuts: Array<{
    id: number;
    panelLayout?: Cut['panelLayout'];
    basicPlot: string;
    narrationJp: string;
    highlights?: string[];
    isObjectOnly?: boolean;
    focalPoint?: FocalPoint;
    compositionPrompt?: string;
    veoMotionPrompt?: string;
    endFramePlot?: string;
  }>;
}
/**
 * 最大20個ずつの「チャンク・リレー生成」ループ
 */
export async function generateSafeEpisodeScript(opts: GenerateSafeScriptOptions): Promise<SafeScriptResult> {
  const {
    epId, currentPlan, country, theme, era, isMangaMode, isMvMode,
    taste, productionMode, existingTitles, characterId, targetCutCount, abortCheck, addLog
  } = opts;
  const totalCuts = targetCutCount && targetCutCount > 0 ? targetCutCount : (isMangaMode ? 24 : (isMvMode ? 30 : 12));
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 20;
  const chunkCount = Math.ceil(totalCuts / CHUNK_SIZE);
  const fixedChar = (characterId && characterId !== 'auto') ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === characterId) : undefined;
  const fallbackDna = fixedChar
    ? fixedChar.dnaPromptEn
    : (isMvMode
      ? 'Consistent 19yo Japanese girl, soft wavy dark brown chin-length bob hair, gentle eyes, oversized cozy knit cardigan, sleek over-ear headphones'
      : 'Consistent single protagonist matching the world setting and narrative');
  let episodeMetadata = {
    titleJp: cleanEpisodeTitle(currentPlan.titleJp || `場面${epId}`),
    titleEn: cleanEpisodeTitleEn(currentPlan.titleEn || `Scene ${epId}`),
    summary: currentPlan.summary || `${currentPlan.titleJp}の世界観で紡がれる物語`,
    characterDna: fallbackDna,
    eraAnalysisJp: '歴史・文化と人情の情景。',
    forbiddenAnachronisms: ['時代にそぐわない現代物'],
    authenticAttireEn: 'Authentic costume matching setting',
    forbiddenKeywordsEn: 'modern items',
    coverCatchphraseJp: `${currentPlan.titleJp}`,
    coverCatchphraseEn: '',
    highlightWords: ['光', '風']
  };
  const accumulatedCuts: SafeScriptResult['cuts'] = [];
  for (let chunkIdx = 0; chunkIdx < chunkCount; chunkIdx++) {
    if (abortCheck && abortCheck()) break;
    if (chunkIdx > 0) {
      if (addLog) addLog(`☕ 次バッチ（Cut ${chunkIdx * CHUNK_SIZE + 1}〜）準備中（API安定のため2秒待機）...`, 'info');
      const rested = await sleepWithAbortCheck(2000, abortCheck);
      if (!rested) break;
    }
    const startCut = chunkIdx * CHUNK_SIZE + 1;
    const endCut = Math.min((chunkIdx + 1) * CHUNK_SIZE, totalCuts);
    const isFirstChunk = chunkIdx === 0;
    let previousCutsSummary = '';
    if (!isFirstChunk && accumulatedCuts.length > 0) {
      previousCutsSummary = accumulatedCuts.map(c => {
        const shortSummary = (c.narrationJp || c.basicPlot || '').replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 30);
        return `#Cut${c.id}: ${shortSummary}`;
      }).join('\n');
    }
    if (addLog) {
      if (chunkCount > 1) {
        addLog(`📝 【第${epId}話】脚本リレー生成中 [${chunkIdx + 1}/${chunkCount}]: Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut} / 全${totalCuts}カット...`, 'process');
      } else {
        addLog(`📝 【第${epId}話】脚本生成中: Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut}...`, 'process');
      }
    }
    const chunkPrompt = buildCompactScriptChunkPrompt(
      epId,
      currentPlan as any,
      startCut,
      endCut,
      totalCuts,
      country,
      theme,
      era,
      isMangaMode,
      isMvMode,
      taste,
      productionMode,
      existingTitles,
      characterId,
      previousCutsSummary
    );
    let scriptRes: any;
    const chunkStartTime = Date.now();
    let scriptHeartbeat: any = null;
    if (addLog) {
      scriptHeartbeat = setInterval(() => {
        const elapsed = Math.round((Date.now() - chunkStartTime) / 1000);
        addLog(`⏳ 【第${epId}話】Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut} 執筆中（${elapsed}秒経過）...`, 'process');
      }, 5000);
    }
    try {
      scriptRes = await callWithRetry<any>(
        () => Flow.generate.text(chunkPrompt),
        (attempt, max, delay, err) => {
          console.error(`[Script Chunk ${chunkIdx + 1} Retry ${attempt}/${max}]`, err);
          const waitStr = formatDurationMs(delay);
          if (addLog) {
            addLog(`⚠️ 第${epId}話 脚本リトライ (${attempt}/${max}) ${waitStr}後...`, 'warning');
          }
        },
        { maxRetries: 3, superBackoff: false, abortCheck }
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn(`[Script Fallback for Ep ${epId} Chunk ${chunkIdx + 1}]`, err);
      if (addLog) addLog(`⚠️ 第${epId}話 Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut}: 自動フェイルセーフ構成を適用します`, 'process');
    } finally {
      if (scriptHeartbeat) clearInterval(scriptHeartbeat);
    }
    let parsedCuts: any[] = [];
    if (scriptRes && scriptRes.text) {
      const parsed = safeJsonParse<any>(scriptRes.text, {});
      if (isFirstChunk) {
        if (parsed.titleJp) episodeMetadata.titleJp = cleanEpisodeTitle(sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(parsed.titleJp));
        if (parsed.titleEn) episodeMetadata.titleEn = cleanEpisodeTitleEn(parsed.titleEn);
        if (parsed.summary) episodeMetadata.summary = sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(parsed.summary);
        if (parsed.characterDna) episodeMetadata.characterDna = parsed.characterDna;
        if (parsed.eraAnalysisJp) episodeMetadata.eraAnalysisJp = sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(parsed.eraAnalysisJp);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.forbiddenAnachronisms)) episodeMetadata.forbiddenAnachronisms = parsed.forbiddenAnachronisms;
        if (parsed.authenticAttireEn) episodeMetadata.authenticAttireEn = parsed.authenticAttireEn;
        if (parsed.forbiddenKeywordsEn) episodeMetadata.forbiddenKeywordsEn = parsed.forbiddenKeywordsEn;
        if (parsed.coverCatchphraseJp) episodeMetadata.coverCatchphraseJp = cleanEpisodeTitle(sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(parsed.coverCatchphraseJp));
        if (parsed.coverCatchphraseEn) episodeMetadata.coverCatchphraseEn = parsed.coverCatchphraseEn;
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.highlightWords)) episodeMetadata.highlightWords = parsed.highlightWords;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.cuts)) {
        parsedCuts = parsed.cuts;
      } else if (Array.isArray(parsed.scenes)) {
        parsedCuts = parsed.scenes;
      } else if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        parsedCuts = parsed;
      } else if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
        if (parsed.cuts && typeof parsed.cuts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed.cuts)) {
          parsedCuts = [parsed.cuts];
        } else if (parsed.basicPlot || parsed.narrationJp || parsed.promptEn || parsed.id) {
          parsedCuts = [parsed];
        }
      }
    }
    const expectedChunkCount = endCut - startCut + 1;
    for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < expectedChunkCount; cIdx++) {
      const globalCutId = startCut + cIdx;
      const rawCutData = parsedCuts.find((c: any) => c && Number(c.id) === globalCutId) || parsedCuts[cIdx] || {};
      const isObj = rawCutData.isObjectOnly !== undefined ? Boolean(rawCutData.isObjectOnly) : (globalCutId % 6 === 0);
      const rawNarration = rawCutData.narrationJp || rawCutData.narration || (isMvMode ? `歌詞パート${globalCutId}` : `場面${globalCutId}の情景`);
      const defaultCoord: [number, number] = isObj ? [0.5, 0.5] : [0.5, 0.3];
      const defaultSubject = isObj ? 'symbolic_object' : 'character_face';
      const defaultPlot = isObj
        ? `Atmospheric iconic object or landscape matching ${theme}, completely empty of people, cinematic still-life`
        : `${fixedChar ? `${fixedChar.dnaPromptEn}, ` : ''}cinematic scene ${globalCutId} of ${currentPlan.titleEn}, atmospheric lighting`;
      accumulatedCuts.push({
        id: globalCutId,
        panelLayout: 'single',
        basicPlot: rawCutData.basicPlot || rawCutData.promptEn || defaultPlot,
        narrationJp: sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(rawNarration),
        highlights: rawCutData.highlights || episodeMetadata.highlightWords || [],
        isObjectOnly: isObj,
        focalPoint: {
          grid: isObj ? 'center' : 'top-center',
          normalizedCoord: defaultCoord,
          focalSubject: defaultSubject
        },
        compositionPrompt: rawCutData.compositionPrompt || (isObj 
          ? 'Centered still-life composition of prop/object, rule of thirds, completely empty of people' 
          : 'Subject framed at upper-third, face centered at top-center, rule of thirds composition')
      });
    }
    if (addLog && chunkCount > 1) {
      addLog(`✅ 【第${epId}話】リレー完了 [${chunkIdx + 1}/${chunkCount}]: Cut ${startCut}〜${endCut} (${accumulatedCuts.length}/${totalCuts}カット確定)`, 'success');
    }
  }
  accumulatedCuts.forEach((cut, idx) => {
    cut.id = idx + 1;
  });
  return {
    ...episodeMetadata,
    cuts: accumulatedCuts
  };
}

/* ========================================================
   FILE: services/coverService.ts
======================================================== */
import { Episode } from '../types';
/**
 * 動画や扉絵用にタイトル文字列をクリーン化
 */
export const cleanTitle = (title: string): string => {
  return (title || '無題').replace(/\s*\(Ep\.\s*\d+\)/gi, '').trim();
};
export interface CoverPalette {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  genre: 'mv' | 'historical' | 'trivia' | 'manzai' | 'cinema';
  vPanelStart: string;
  vPanelEnd: string;
  hPanelStart: string;
  hPanelMid: string;
  hPanelEnd: string;
  borderColors: [string, string, string];
  ribbonColors: [string, string];
  badgeColor: string;
}
export const COVER_PALETTES: Record<string, CoverPalette> = {
  cinema_indigo: {
    id: 'cinema_indigo',
    name: 'ロイヤルインディゴ',
    genre: 'cinema',
    vPanelStart: '#0D0D12',
    vPanelEnd: '#181822',
    hPanelStart: 'rgba(24, 24, 34, 0.98)',
    hPanelMid: 'rgba(13, 13, 18, 0.95)',
    hPanelEnd: 'rgba(8, 8, 10, 0.9)',
    borderColors: ['#E5E7EB', '#6366F1', '#E5E7EB'],
    ribbonColors: ['#4338CA', '#1E1B4B'],
    badgeColor: '#A5B4FC'
  }
};
/**
 * AIが世界観に合わせて直接看板を描き込んだ9:16縦ロング扉絵イラストのBase64を取得
 */
export async function getCleanCoverBase64(ep: Episode): Promise<string | null> {
  const raw = ep.coverBase64 || ep.masterAnchorBase64 || ep.cuts?.[0]?.imageBase64;
  if (!raw) return null;
  return raw.startsWith('data:') ? raw : `data:image/png;base64,${raw}`;
}
/**
 * AIが世界観に合わせて直接看板を描き込んだ16:9横型マスターカバー（横動画サムネイル）イラストのBase64を取得
 */
export async function getCleanCoverBase64Horizontal(ep: Episode): Promise<string | null> {
  const raw = ep.coverHorizontalBase64 || ep.masterAnchorBase64 || ep.cuts?.[0]?.imageBase64;
  if (!raw) return null;
  return raw.startsWith('data:') ? raw : `data:image/png;base64,${raw}`;
}
/**
 * 縦型扉絵: 暗いフィルター合成を廃止し、生成イラストをそのまま返却
 */
export async function renderCoverBase64(ep: Episode): Promise<string> {
  const cleanUri = await getCleanCoverBase64(ep);
  if (cleanUri) return cleanUri;
  return '';
}
/**
 * 横型16:9マスターカバー: 暗いフィルターや2重文字描画を廃止し、生成された明るい16:9イラストをそのまま返却
 */
export async function renderCoverBase64Horizontal(ep: Episode): Promise<string> {
  const cleanUri = await getCleanCoverBase64Horizontal(ep);
  if (cleanUri) return cleanUri;
  // 16:9がまだ無ければ縦扉絵をフォールバック
  return await renderCoverBase64(ep);
}

/* ========================================================
   FILE: components/CoverPreviewModal.tsx
======================================================== */
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Episode } from '../types';
import { renderCoverBase64, renderCoverBase64Horizontal, COVER_PALETTES, CoverPalette } from '../services/coverService';
import { Flow } from 'flow-sdk';
interface CoverPreviewModalProps {
  episode: Episode | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdateEpisodePalette?: (paletteId: string) => void;
}
export const CoverPreviewModal: React.FC<CoverPreviewModalProps> = ({
  episode,
  onClose,
  onUpdateEpisodePalette
}) => {
  const [orientation, setOrientation] = useState<'vertical' | 'horizontal'>('vertical');
  const [renderedBase64, setRenderedBase64] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isRendering, setIsRendering] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [selectedPaletteId, setSelectedPaletteId] = useState<string>(episode?.coverThemeVariant || 'cinema_indigo');
  const [downloadState, setDownloadState] = useState<'idle' | 'downloading' | 'done' | 'error'>('idle');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!episode) return;
    let isCancelled = false;
    const generateCover = async () => {
      setIsRendering(true);
      try {
        const epWithPalette = { ...episode, coverThemeVariant: selectedPaletteId };
        const b64 = orientation === 'vertical'
          ? await renderCoverBase64(epWithPalette)
          : await renderCoverBase64Horizontal(epWithPalette);
        if (!isCancelled) {
          setRenderedBase64(b64);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Failed to render cover:', e);
      } finally {
        if (!isCancelled) {
          setIsRendering(false);
        }
      }
    };
    generateCover();
    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
    };
  }, [episode, orientation, selectedPaletteId]);
  if (!episode) return null;
  const handlePaletteSelect = (palette: CoverPalette) => {
    setSelectedPaletteId(palette.id);
    if (onUpdateEpisodePalette) {
      onUpdateEpisodePalette(palette.id);
    }
  };
  const handleDownload = async () => {
    if (!renderedBase64) return;
    setDownloadState('downloading');
    try {
      const cleanBase64 = renderedBase64.includes(',') ? renderedBase64.split(',')[1] : renderedBase64;
      const filename = `cover_${episode.id}_${orientation}.jpg`;
      await Flow.download({
        base64: cleanBase64,
        mimeType: 'image/jpeg',
        filename,
      });
      setDownloadState('done');
      setTimeout(() => setDownloadState('idle'), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Cover download error:', err);
      setDownloadState('error');
      setTimeout(() => setDownloadState('idle'), 3000);
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
        {/* ヘッダー */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined text-indigo-400">art_track</span>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100">
              インフォグラフィック看板 / 扉絵ジェネレーター
            </h2>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 transition-colors p-1"
          >
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined text-lg">close</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        {/* コントロールバー */}
        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-950/70 border-b border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">アスペクト:</span>
            <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-900 p-0.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-800">
              <button
                onClick={() => setOrientation('vertical')}
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  orientation === 'vertical'
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white font-semibold'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">stay_current_portrait</span>
                9:16 (縦型Shorts)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setOrientation('horizontal')}
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  orientation === 'horizontal'
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white font-semibold'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">stay_current_landscape</span>
                16:9 (横型シネマ)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleDownload}
              disabled={!renderedBase64 || isRendering || downloadState !== 'idle'}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
            >
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">
                {downloadState === 'downloading' ? 'hourglass_top' : downloadState === 'done' ? 'check' : 'download'}
              </span>
              {downloadState === 'downloading' ? 'ダウンロード中...' : downloadState === 'done' ? '完了 ✓' : '高解像度保存'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* プレビュー本体 & パレットセレクター */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-5 items-center justify-center bg-slate-950">
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center min-h-[360px] w-full">
            {isRendering ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-indigo-400">
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-3xl">progress_activity</span>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono">看板ポスターをレンダリング中...</span>
              </div>
            ) : renderedBase64 ? (
              <div className="relative group max-h-[60vh] flex items-center justify-center">
                <img
                  src={renderedBase64}
                  alt="Rendered Cover"
                  className={`rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-800 object-contain max-h-[60vh] ${
                    orientation === 'vertical' ? 'aspect-[9/16]' : 'aspect-video'
                  }`}
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-slate-600 text-xs">プレビューの準備ができていません</div>
            )}
          </div>
          {/* 右サイド：パレット切り替え */}
          <div className="w-full md:w-64 flex flex-col gap-2 shrink-0 border-t md:border-t-0 md:border-l border-slate-800 pt-3 md:pt-0 md:pl-4">
            <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
              看板カラーパレット
            </label>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-[50vh] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {Object.values(COVER_PALETTES).map((pal) => {
                const isSelected = selectedPaletteId === pal.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={pal.id}
                    onClick={() => handlePaletteSelect(pal)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 p-2 rounded-lg text-left transition-all border text-xs ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-indigo-950/60 border-indigo-500 text-white shadow-sm'
                        : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:border-slate-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full border border-white/20 shrink-0"
                      style={{ background: pal.vPanelEnd }}
                    />
                    <div className="flex-1 truncate">
                      <div className="font-medium truncate">{pal.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 capitalize">{pal.genre}</div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import { extractImageBase64 } from './utils';

/**
 * 循環参照やDOMノード・クロスオリジンWindowを確実に排除する安全なJSON文字列化関数
 */
export const safeJsonStringify = (obj: any, indent = 2): string => {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  const sanitizeValue = (val: any): any => {
    if (val === null || val === undefined) return val;
    if (typeof val === 'function' || typeof val === 'symbol') return undefined;
    if (typeof val !== 'object') return val;
    try {
      if (val === window || (typeof Window !== 'undefined' && val instanceof Window)) return undefined;
      if (typeof Event !== 'undefined' && val instanceof Event) return undefined;
      if (typeof val.nodeType === 'number' || val.$$typeof || val.nativeEvent) return undefined;
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    }
    if (seen.has(val)) return '[Circular]';
    seen.add(val);
    if (Array.isArray(val)) {
      return val.map(item => sanitizeValue(item));
    }
    const cleanObj: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(val)) {
      try {
        const child = sanitizeValue(val[k]);
        if (child !== undefined) {
          cleanObj[k] = child;
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
    return cleanObj;
  };
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(sanitizeValue(obj), null, indent);
  } catch (err) {
    return '{}';
  }
};
/**
 * 秒数を SRT タイムコード形式 (HH:MM:SS,mmm) に変換
 */
function formatSrtTime(seconds: number): string {
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
  const millis = Math.floor((seconds % 1) * 1000);
  const pad = (n: number, z = 2) => String(n).padStart(z, '0');
  return `${pad(hrs)}:${pad(mins)}:${pad(secs)},${pad(millis, 3)}`;
}
/**
 * エピソードのカット一覧から標準 subtitles.srt を生成
 */
export function generateSrtContent(ep: Episode, cutDurationSeconds = 4): string {
  const cuts = ep.cuts || [];
  let srt = '';
  let currentTime = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length; i++) {
    const cut = cuts[i];
    const duration = cut.duration || cutDurationSeconds;
    const startTime = currentTime;
    const endTime = startTime + duration;
    currentTime = endTime;
    const text = cut.narrationJp || cut.scenePlot || `Cut ${cut.id}`;
    srt += `${i + 1}\n`;
    srt += `${formatSrtTime(startTime)} --> ${formatSrtTime(endTime)}\n`;
    srt += `${text.trim()}\n\n`;
  }
  return srt;
}
/**
 * ファイル名を生成（ASCII安全名とサニタイズ表示名の両方を作成）
 * ファイル名の先頭に主人公キャラクター名とシリーズ共通4桁ハッシュ（例: [ずんだもん]_A7F2_001_...）を付与
 * ファイル名先頭の3桁連番は実際のエピソード番号（ep.id）に基づく
 * 全カット画像が揃っている場合は「_full」、未生成カットがある場合は「_preview」を付与
 */
export function generateSafeFilenames(ep: Episode): { asciiFilename: string; displayFilename: string } {
  const charId = ep.characterId || 'zundamon';
  const foundChar = SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === charId);
  const charNameJa = foundChar?.nameJa || (charId === 'zundamon' ? 'ずんだもん' : charId);
  const hashChars = '23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ';
  let seriesHash = (ep as any).seriesHash || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? (window as any).__STUDIO_SERIES_HASH__ : '');
  if (!seriesHash) {
    seriesHash = Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => hashChars[Math.floor(Math.random() * hashChars.length)]).join('');
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      (window as any).__STUDIO_SERIES_HASH__ = seriesHash;
    }
  }
  const charPrefix = `[${charNameJa}]_${seriesHash}_`;
  const charAsciiPrefix = `[${foundChar?.id || charId}]_${seriesHash}_`;
  // 実際のエピソード番号（String(ep.id).padStart(3, '0')）を使用
  const epNumStr = String(ep.id || 1).padStart(3, '0');
  const now = new Date();
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  const timestamp = `${now.getFullYear()}${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(now.getDate())}_${pad(now.getHours())}${pad(now.getMinutes())}${pad(now.getSeconds())}`;
  
  const cuts = ep.cuts || [];
  const isFull = cuts.length > 0 && cuts.every(c => !!(c.imageBase64 || c.imageMediaId));
  const completionSuffix = isFull ? '_full' : '_preview';
  const asciiFilename = `${charAsciiPrefix}Ep${epNumStr}_${timestamp}${completionSuffix}.zip`;
  const cleanTitle = (ep.titleJp || 'Episode')
    .replace(/[\uD800-\uDBFF][\uDC00-\uDFFF]/g, '')
    .replace(/[\u2600-\u27BF\uE000-\uF8FF]/g, '')
    .replace(/[「」『』【】（）()［］\[\]・…！？!?,:;~〜\/\:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
    .replace(/\s+/g, '_')
    .replace(/_+/g, '_')
    .trim()
    .slice(0, 30);
  const displayFilename = `${charPrefix}${epNumStr}_${timestamp}_${cleanTitle || `Ep${ep.id}`}${completionSuffix}.zip`;
  return { asciiFilename, displayFilename };
}
/**
 * あらゆるブラウザ環境・Google Flow sandbox 環境でファイルを確実に保存する統合関数
 */
export const savePackageFile = async (
  blob: Blob,
  filename: string,
  addLog?: (msg: string, type?: any) => void,
  fallbackAsciiFilename?: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; method: string }> => {
  const sizeMb = (blob.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2);
  const sizeStr = `${sizeMb} MB`;
  // 手法 1: File System Access API
  if (typeof (window as any).showSaveFilePicker === 'function') {
    try {
      const handle = await (window as any).showSaveFilePicker({
        suggestedName: filename,
        types: [{
          description: 'ZIP Package Archive',
          accept: { 'application/zip': ['.zip'] }
        }]
      });
      const writable = await handle.createWritable();
      await writable.write(blob);
      await writable.close();
      if (addLog) addLog(`✅ ファイル「${filename}」(${sizeStr}) を保存しました (File System Access)。`, 'success');
      return { success: true, method: 'showSaveFilePicker' };
    } catch (fsErr: any) {
      if (fsErr.name === 'AbortError') {
        return { success: false, method: 'user_cancelled' };
      }
    }
  }
  // 手法 2: Google Flow Tools 公式 Flow.download API (text/plain .zip.txt で直接ダウンロード)
  if (typeof Flow !== 'undefined' && typeof Flow.download === 'function') {
    try {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      const base64 = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
        reader.onloadend = () => resolve((reader.result as string).split(',')[1]);
        reader.onerror = reject;
        reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
      });
      const baseClean = filename.replace(/\.zip$/i, '').replace(/[\uD800-\uDBFF][\uDC00-\uDFFF]/g, '').replace(/[「」『』]/g, '_').trim();
      const txtFallbackFilename = `${baseClean || 'FlowTool_Package'}.zip.txt`;
      await Flow.download({ base64, mimeType: 'text/plain', filename: txtFallbackFilename });
      if (addLog) {
        addLog(`✅ パッケージ「${txtFallbackFilename}」(${sizeStr}) をダウンロード完了！`, 'success');
      }
      return { success: true, method: 'Flow.download.txt' };
    } catch (_) {}
  }
  // 手法 3: DOM <a download>
  try {
    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = blobUrl;
    a.download = filename;
    a.target = '_blank';
    a.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
    a.style.display = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        if (a.parentNode) document.body.removeChild(a);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
      } catch (_) {}
    }, 10000);
  } catch (_) {}
  if (addLog) addLog(`💾 パッケージ「${filename}」(${sizeStr}) の保存コマンドを送信しました。`, 'info');
  return { success: true, method: 'dom_dispatched' };
};
/**
 * series_manifest.json 単体の自動ダウンロード（ZIP同梱のみ）
 */
export const downloadManifestFile = async (_manifest: SeriesManifest, _addLog: (msg: string, type?: any) => void) => {};
/**
 * エピソード全体を完全なZIPアーカイブ（画像・台本・メタデータ・cover_v.png・cover_h.png・script.json・subtitles.srt）としてパッケージ化
 */
export async function packageEpisodeToZip(
  ep: Episode,
  _settings: any,
  addLog: (msg: string, type?: any) => void,
  _logs: LogEntry[] = [],
  _allEpisodes: Episode[] = []
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; asciiFilename: string; displayFilename: string; base64Zip?: string }> {
  addLog(`📦 【第${ep.id}話】ZIPアーカイブの構築を開始します...`, 'process');
  const zip = new JSZip();
  const folder = zip.folder(`Episode_${ep.id}`) || zip;
  // 1. 各カット画像
  const cuts = ep.cuts || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length; i++) {
    const cut = cuts[i];
    const cutNum = String(cut.id || i + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    if (cut.imageBase64) {
      const ext = extractImageBase64(cut.imageBase64) || cut.imageBase64;
      const clean = ext.split('base64,').pop() || ext;
      folder.file(`cut_${cutNum}.png`, clean, { base64: true });
    }
    if (cut.endFrameImageBase64) {
      const extEnd = extractImageBase64(cut.endFrameImageBase64) || cut.endFrameImageBase64;
      const cleanEnd = extEnd.split('base64,').pop() || extEnd;
      folder.file(`cut_${cutNum}_end.png`, cleanEnd, { base64: true });
    }
  }
  // 2. カバー画像の整理：cover_v.png（9:16縦）と cover_h.png（16:9横）
  const coverVRaw = ep.coverBase64 || ep.masterAnchorBase64 || ep.cuts?.[0]?.imageBase64;
  if (coverVRaw) {
    const cleanCoverV = coverVRaw.split('base64,').pop() || coverVRaw;
    folder.file('cover_v.png', cleanCoverV, { base64: true });
  }
  const coverHRaw = ep.coverHorizontalBase64 || ep.masterAnchorBase64 || ep.cuts?.[0]?.imageBase64;
  if (coverHRaw) {
    const cleanCoverH = coverHRaw.split('base64,').pop() || coverHRaw;
    folder.file('cover_h.png', cleanCoverH, { base64: true });
  }
  // 3. キャラクター三面図
  if (ep.characterTurnaroundBase64) {
    const cleanTurnaround = ep.characterTurnaroundBase64.split('base64,').pop() || ep.characterTurnaroundBase64;
    folder.file('character_turnaround.png', cleanTurnaround, { base64: true });
  }
  // 4. 台本テキスト (scenario.txt)
  let scriptText = `【作品タイトル】${ep.titleJp || '無題'}\n【英語タイトル】${ep.titleEn || ''}\n【テーマ】${ep.theme || ''}\n【時代・世界観】${ep.era || ''}\n\n`;
  for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length; i++) {
    const c = cuts[i];
    scriptText += `--- CUT ${c.id} ---\n`;
    scriptText += `【演出指示】${c.scenePlot || c.cleanAction || ''}\n`;
    scriptText += `【台詞・ナレーション】${c.narrationJp || ''}\n`;
    scriptText += `【英語プロンプト】${c.promptEn || ''}\n\n`;
  }
  folder.file('scenario.txt', scriptText);
  // 🎙️ VOICEVOX スピーカーID（voice_name）のメタデータ解決
  const VOICE_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
    zundamon: 'voicevox:3',   // ずんだもん (ノーマル)
    metan: 'voicevox:2',      // 四国めたん (ノーマル)
    tsumugi: 'voicevox:8',    // 春日部つむぎ
    himari: 'voicevox:14',    // 冥鳴ひまり
  };
  const resolvedVoiceName = VOICE_MAP[ep.characterId || ''] || 'voicevox:3';
  const resolvedSpeaker = ep.characterId || 'zundamon';
  // 5. 後段ツール・動画化スクリプト連携用 構造化スクリプト (script.json)
  const structuredScript = {
    episodeId: ep.id,
    titleJp: ep.titleJp,
    titleEn: ep.titleEn,
    summary: ep.summary,
    theme: ep.theme,
    era: ep.era,
    productionMode: ep.productionMode,
    characterDna: ep.characterDna,
    voice_name: resolvedVoiceName,
    speaker: resolvedSpeaker,
    cuts: cuts.map((c, idx) => ({
      cutNumber: c.id || idx + 1,
      narrationJp: c.narrationJp || '',
      scenePlot: c.scenePlot || c.cleanAction || '',
      promptEn: c.promptEn || '',
      shotScale: c.shotScale || '',
      cinematicAngle: c.cinematicAngle || '',
      isObjectOnly: Boolean(c.isObjectOnly),
      durationSeconds: c.duration || 4,
      telop: c.telop || { fullText: c.narrationJp || '' },
      cut_no: c.id || idx + 1,
      narration: c.narrationJp || '',
      dialogue: c.narrationJp || '',
      tts_text: c.narrationJp || '',
      visual_prompt: c.promptEn || '',
      duration: c.duration || 4
    }))
  };
  folder.file('script.json', safeJsonStringify(structuredScript, 2));
  // 6. 字幕ファイル (subtitles.srt)
  const srtContent = generateSrtContent(ep, 4);
  folder.file('subtitles.srt', srtContent);
  // 7. 設定メタデータ (series_manifest.json) を同梱
  const manifestData = {
    episodeId: ep.id,
    titleJp: ep.titleJp,
    titleEn: ep.titleEn,
    theme: ep.theme,
    era: ep.era,
    productionMode: ep.productionMode,
    cutsCount: cuts.length,
    characterDna: ep.characterDna,
    voice_name: resolvedVoiceName,
    speaker: resolvedSpeaker,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  folder.file('series_manifest.json', safeJsonStringify(manifestData, 2));
  // 8. ZIPバイナリ生成
  addLog(`⚙️ ZIPアーカイブを圧縮中...`, 'process');
  const blob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob', compression: 'DEFLATE', compressionOptions: { level: 6 } });
  const { asciiFilename, displayFilename } = generateSafeFilenames(ep);
  addLog(`✅ ZIPアーカイブの構築が完了しました（${(blob.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB）`, 'success');
  return { blob, asciiFilename, displayFilename };
}
/**
 * エピソード全体のアセットを同梱したZIPパッケージを作成・ダウンロード保存
 */
export const downloadZip = async (
  ep: Episode, 
  addLog: (msg: string, type?: any) => void, 
  _manifest?: SeriesManifest,
  logs?: LogEntry[]
): Promise<{ filename: string; blobUrl: string; sizeStr: string; flowSuccess: boolean } | null> => {
  try {
    const { blob, asciiFilename, displayFilename } = await packageEpisodeToZip(ep, {}, addLog, logs);
    const result = await savePackageFile(blob, displayFilename, addLog, asciiFilename);
    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    return {
      filename: displayFilename,
      blobUrl,
      sizeStr: `${(blob.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`,
      flowSuccess: result.success
    };
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
};

/**
 * ── 1. パイプライン内カット生成ファクトリ ──
 */
export function createPipelineCut(
  cutIndex: number,
  cutData: any,
  sharedScript: any,
  settings: GeneratorSettings,
  effectiveMode: string,
  isMv: boolean,
  isManga: boolean
): Cut {
  const narration = cutData.narrationJp || '';
  const plot = cutData.basicPlot || '';
  const cut = createDefaultCut(cutIndex + 1, narration, plot, false);
  cut.panelLayout = 'single';
  const preset = getStoryboardPreset(cutIndex + 1, isMv, isManga);
  cut.shotScale = preset.scale;
  cut.cinematicAngle = preset.angle;
  const cwDef = resolveRecommendedCameraWorkAndKenBurns(cutIndex + 1, effectiveMode, isMv, isManga);
  cut.cameraWork = cwDef.id;
  cut.cameraMotion = cwDef.motionPrompt;
  cut.kenBurnsPreset = cwDef.recommendedKenBurns;
  const dramaStaging = resolveRecommendedTelopStaging(cutIndex + 1, isMv, false, undefined, effectiveMode);
  Object.assign(cut.telop, dramaStaging);
  cut.telop.highlights = extractHighlights(narration, cutData.highlights || sharedScript.highlightWords);
  cut.isObjectOnly = settings.enableObjectCut !== false ? (cutData.isObjectOnly || false) : false;
  cut.focalPoint = cutData.focalPoint;
  cut.compositionPrompt = cutData.compositionPrompt;
  cut.veoMotionPrompt = cutData.veoMotionPrompt;
  cut.endFramePromptEn = cutData.endFramePlot;
  return cut;
}
/**
 * ── 2. 主人公キャラクターの三面図マスターシート生成 ──
 */
export async function ensureCharacterTurnaround(
  ctx: ProductionPipelineContext,
  ep: Episode
): Promise<{ mediaId?: string; base64?: string }> {
  const { settings, addLog, activeReferenceRef } = ctx;
  if (ep.characterTurnaroundMediaId) {
    return { mediaId: ep.characterTurnaroundMediaId, base64: ep.characterTurnaroundBase64 };
  }
  if (!ep.isMvMode && !settings.isMvMode && activeReferenceRef.current?.mediaId) {
    return { mediaId: activeReferenceRef.current.mediaId };
  }
  if (settings.enableTurnaroundSheet === false) return {};
  try {
    const charId = ep.characterId || settings.characterId;
    const fixedChar = (charId && charId !== 'auto') ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === charId) : undefined;
    addLog(`🎨 【三面図マスター生成】主人公（${fixedChar ? fixedChar.nameJa : '主人公'}）の三面図を設計中...`, 'process');
    const turnaroundPrompt = buildCharacterTurnaroundPrompt(
      ep.theme || settings.theme,
      settings.country,
      ep.era || settings.era,
      ep.taste || settings.taste,
      fixedChar?.dnaPromptEn || ep.characterDna,
      charId
    );
    const modelDef = resolveImageModel(settings.imageModel);
    const safePrompt = turnaroundPrompt && /[a-zA-Z0-9]/.test(turnaroundPrompt) ? turnaroundPrompt : 'Character turnaround sheet, front side back views';
    let safeModelName = modelDef?.name || '🍌 Nano Banana Pro';
    let res: any;
    try {
      res = await callWithRetry<any>(
        () => Flow.generate.image({
          prompt: safePrompt,
          modelDisplayName: safeModelName,
          aspectRatio: '16:9' as any
        }),
        (attempt, max, delay, err) => {
          const errMsg = formatErrorMessage(err);
          if (errMsg.includes('使用量の上限') || errMsg.includes('429') || errMsg.includes('RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED')) {
            safeModelName = '🍌 Nano Banana Pro';
            addLog(`👑 【三面図】上限検知！「🍌 Nano Banana Pro」へ自動昇格します`, 'success');
          }
        },
      );
    } catch (_) {
      safeModelName = '🍌 Nano Banana Pro';
      res = await Flow.generate.image({
        prompt: safePrompt,
        modelDisplayName: safeModelName,
        aspectRatio: '16:9' as any
      });
    }
    let base64 = extractImageBase64(res);
    const mediaId = extractMediaId(res);
    if (!base64 && mediaId && typeof (Flow as any)?.media?.getBase64 === 'function') {
      try { base64 = extractImageBase64(await (Flow as any).media.getBase64(mediaId)); } catch (_) {}
    }
    if (mediaId || base64) {
      addLog(`✨ 【三面図マスター確定】主人公の三面図シートが完成！全カットの基準アセットとして登録しました。`, 'success');
      return { mediaId, base64 };
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    addLog(`⚠️ 三面図の自動生成をスキップし通常描画を継続します: ${formatErrorMessage(err)}`, 'warning');
  }
  return {};
}
/**
 * ── 3. 世界観適応 9:16縦型扉絵 ＆ 16:9横型マスターカバーの自動生成 ──
 */
export async function ensureInfographicCover(
  ctx: ProductionPipelineContext,
  ep: Episode,
  force = false
): Promise<{ 
  coverVertical?: { mediaId?: string; base64?: string };
  coverHorizontal?: { mediaId?: string; base64?: string };
}> {
  const { settings, addLog, isAbortedRef, activeReferenceRef, updateEpisode, episodesRef, setEpisodes } = ctx;
  if (isAbortedRef.current) return {};
  const freshEp = episodesRef.current.find((e: Episode) => e.id === ep.id) || ep;
  const hasVertical = !!(freshEp.coverMediaId || freshEp.coverBase64);
  const hasHorizontal = !!(freshEp.coverHorizontalMediaId || freshEp.coverHorizontalBase64);
  if (!force && hasVertical && hasHorizontal) {
    return {
      coverVertical: { mediaId: freshEp.coverMediaId, base64: freshEp.coverBase64 },
      coverHorizontal: { mediaId: freshEp.coverHorizontalMediaId, base64: freshEp.coverHorizontalBase64 }
    };
  }
  try {
    addLog(`🎨 Ep.${freshEp.id}: 【扉絵＆16:9看板生成】世界観適応の9:16縦扉絵および16:9マスターカバーを描画中...`, 'process');
    updateEpisode(freshEp.id, { isGeneratingCover: true });
    const charId = freshEp.characterId || settings.characterId;
    const fixedChar = (charId && charId !== 'auto') ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === charId) : undefined;
    
    const refMediaIds: string[] = [];
    if (freshEp.masterAnchorMediaId) {
      refMediaIds.push(freshEp.masterAnchorMediaId);
    } else if (freshEp.characterTurnaroundMediaId) {
      refMediaIds.push(freshEp.characterTurnaroundMediaId);
    } else if (activeReferenceRef.current?.mediaId) {
      refMediaIds.push(activeReferenceRef.current.mediaId);
    }
    const modelDef = resolveImageModel(settings.imageModel);
    let safeModelName = modelDef?.name || '🍌 Nano Banana Pro';
    let verticalMediaId = freshEp.coverMediaId;
    let verticalBase64 = freshEp.coverBase64;
    let verticalPromptEn = freshEp.coverPromptEn;
    let horizontalMediaId = freshEp.coverHorizontalMediaId;
    let horizontalBase64 = freshEp.coverHorizontalBase64;
    let horizontalPromptEn = freshEp.coverHorizontalPromptEn;
    // A. 9:16 縦ロング扉絵の生成
    if (force || !hasVertical) {
      const coverPromptObj = buildAdaptiveInfographicCoverPrompt({
        titleJp: cleanEpisodeTitle(freshEp.titleJp),
        titleEn: cleanEpisodeTitleEn(freshEp.titleEn),
        coverCatchphraseJp: cleanEpisodeTitle(freshEp.coverCatchphraseJp || ''),
        coverCatchphraseEn: cleanEpisodeTitleEn(freshEp.coverCatchphraseEn || ''),
        theme: freshEp.theme || settings.theme,
        era: freshEp.era || settings.era,
        taste: freshEp.taste || settings.taste,
        country: settings.country,
        productionMode: freshEp.productionMode || settings.productionMode,
        characterDna: fixedChar?.dnaPromptEn || freshEp.characterDna,
        styleDna: activeReferenceRef.current?.styleDna,
        forbiddenAnachronisms: freshEp.forbiddenAnachronisms,
        authenticAttireEn: freshEp.authenticAttireEn,
        isHorizontal: false
      });
      verticalPromptEn = coverPromptObj.promptEn;
      try {
        const resV: any = await Flow.generate.image({
          prompt: coverPromptObj.promptEn,
          modelDisplayName: safeModelName,
          aspectRatio: '9:16' as any,
          referenceImageMediaIds: refMediaIds.length > 0 ? refMediaIds : undefined
        });
        verticalBase64 = extractImageBase64(resV);
        verticalMediaId = extractMediaId(resV);
        if (!verticalBase64 && verticalMediaId && typeof (Flow as any)?.media?.getBase64 === 'function') {
          try { verticalBase64 = extractImageBase64(await (Flow as any).media.getBase64(verticalMediaId)); } catch (_) {}
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn('Vertical cover gen error:', err);
      }
    }
    // B. 16:9 横型マスターカバー（横動画サムネイル看板）の生成
    if (force || !hasHorizontal) {
      const coverPromptH = buildAdaptiveInfographicCoverPrompt({
        titleJp: cleanEpisodeTitle(freshEp.titleJp),
        titleEn: cleanEpisodeTitleEn(freshEp.titleEn),
        coverCatchphraseJp: cleanEpisodeTitle(freshEp.coverCatchphraseJp || ''),
        coverCatchphraseEn: cleanEpisodeTitleEn(freshEp.coverCatchphraseEn || ''),
        theme: freshEp.theme || settings.theme,
        era: freshEp.era || settings.era,
        taste: freshEp.taste || settings.taste,
        country: settings.country,
        productionMode: freshEp.productionMode || settings.productionMode,
        characterDna: fixedChar?.dnaPromptEn || freshEp.characterDna,
        styleDna: activeReferenceRef.current?.styleDna,
        forbiddenAnachronisms: freshEp.forbiddenAnachronisms,
        authenticAttireEn: freshEp.authenticAttireEn,
        isHorizontal: true
      });
      horizontalPromptEn = coverPromptH.promptEn;
      try {
        const resH: any = await Flow.generate.image({
          prompt: coverPromptH.promptEn,
          modelDisplayName: safeModelName,
          aspectRatio: '16:9' as any,
          referenceImageMediaIds: refMediaIds.length > 0 ? refMediaIds : undefined
        });
        horizontalBase64 = extractImageBase64(resH);
        horizontalMediaId = extractMediaId(resH);
        if (!horizontalBase64 && horizontalMediaId && typeof (Flow as any)?.media?.getBase64 === 'function') {
          try { horizontalBase64 = extractImageBase64(await (Flow as any).media.getBase64(horizontalMediaId)); } catch (_) {}
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn('Horizontal cover gen error:', err);
      }
    }
    const updates = {
      coverMediaId: verticalMediaId,
      coverBase64: verticalBase64,
      coverPromptEn: verticalPromptEn,
      coverHorizontalMediaId: horizontalMediaId,
      coverHorizontalBase64: horizontalBase64,
      coverHorizontalPromptEn: horizontalPromptEn,
      isGeneratingCover: false
    };
    updateEpisode(freshEp.id, updates);
    episodesRef.current = episodesRef.current.map((e: Episode) => e.id === freshEp.id ? { ...e, ...updates } : e);
    setEpisodes((prev: Episode[]) => prev.map((e: Episode) => e.id === freshEp.id ? { ...e, ...updates } : e));
    addLog(`✨ Ep.${freshEp.id}: 【看板完成】9:16縦扉絵および16:9横型マスターカバーが完成しました！`, 'success');
    return {
      coverVertical: { mediaId: verticalMediaId, base64: verticalBase64 },
      coverHorizontal: { mediaId: horizontalMediaId, base64: horizontalBase64 }
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    updateEpisode(freshEp.id, { isGeneratingCover: false });
    addLog(`⚠️ Ep.${freshEp.id}: 扉絵・16:9看板生成スキップ: ${formatErrorMessage(err)}`, 'warning');
  }
  return {};
}
/**
 * ── 4. 1話完結ライフサイクル共通モジュール ──
 */
export interface FinalizeEpisodeOptions {
  unitLabel?: string;
  title?: string;
  manifest?: SeriesManifest | null;
  onDownloaded?: (blobUrl: string, filename: string, sizeStr: string) => void;
}
export async function finalizeAndProcessEpisode(
  ctx: ProductionPipelineContext,
  epId: number,
  options?: FinalizeEpisodeOptions
): Promise<void> {
  const { settings, addLog, isAbortedRef, episodesRef, setEpisodes, updateEpisode } = ctx;
  const unitLabel = options?.unitLabel || '話';
  if (isAbortedRef.current) return;
  const currentEp = episodesRef.current.find((e: Episode) => Number(e.id) === Number(epId));
  if (!currentEp) return;
  const epTitle = options?.title || currentEp.titleJp || `第${epId}${unitLabel}`;
  const allCutsCompleted = currentEp.cuts.every(c => !!(c.imageBase64 || c.imageMediaId));
  const stateUpdate = {
    isGenerating: false,
    isBatchGeneratingVideos: false,
    isPreviewDone: true,
    isDone: allCutsCompleted
  };
  updateEpisode(epId, stateUpdate);
  episodesRef.current = episodesRef.current.map((e: Episode) => Number(e.id) === Number(epId) ? {
    ...e, ...stateUpdate, cuts: e.cuts.map(c => ({ ...c, isGeneratingVideo: false }))
  } : e);
  setEpisodes((prev: Episode[]) => prev.map((e: Episode) => Number(e.id) === Number(epId) ? {
    ...e, ...stateUpdate, cuts: e.cuts.map(c => ({ ...c, isGeneratingVideo: false }))
  } : e));
  const manifest = options?.manifest || ctx.seriesManifestRef?.current;
  if (manifest && allCutsCompleted) {
    manifest.completedEpisodeIds = Array.from(new Set([...(manifest.completedEpisodeIds || []), epId]));
    manifest.currentEpisodeId = epId + 1;
    if (ctx.setActiveSeriesManifest) {
      ctx.setActiveSeriesManifest({ ...manifest });
    }
  }
  const freshEp = episodesRef.current.find((e: Episode) => Number(e.id) === Number(epId)) || currentEp;
  // ■ 4. 先行カット待機時の「早すぎるZIP自動ダウンロード」の防止
  // 全カットが描き切れた（allCutsCompleted === true）場合にのみ自動ダウンロードを発火
  if (settings.autoDownload && !isAbortedRef.current && allCutsCompleted) {
    addLog(`📦 【第${epId}${unitLabel}】『${epTitle}』全カット完成！自動ZIPパッケージングを開始...`, 'process');
    const currentLogs = ctx.logsRef?.current || ((typeof window !== 'undefined' && (window as any).__STUDIO_LOGS__) || []);
    try {
      const res = await downloadZip(freshEp, addLog, manifest || undefined, currentLogs);
      if (res) {
        if (freshEp.packageZipBlobUrl) {
          try { URL.revokeObjectURL(freshEp.packageZipBlobUrl); } catch (_) {}
        }
        updateEpisode(epId, {
          packageZipBlobUrl: res.blobUrl,
          packageZipFilename: res.filename,
          packageZipSizeStr: res.sizeStr
        });
        if (options?.onDownloaded) {
          options.onDownloaded(res.blobUrl, res.filename, res.sizeStr);
        }
        addLog(`💾 【第${epId}${unitLabel}】『${epTitle}』完成版ZIPパッケージの保存が完了しました（${res.sizeStr}）！`, 'success');
      }
    } catch (zipErr) {
      console.error(`Failed to auto-download zip for episode ${epId}:`, zipErr);
      addLog(`⚠️ 第${epId}${unitLabel}の自動ダウンロード中に一時的エラーが発生しました`, 'warning');
    }
  } else if (!allCutsCompleted) {
    addLog(`⏸️ 【第${epId}${unitLabel}】『${epTitle}』先行確認完了（残りカットはSTANDBY待機のため、ZIP自動保存は全カット描画完了まで保留します）。`, 'info');
  }
  cleanupEpisodeMemory();
  addLog(`🏁 【第${epId}${unitLabel}】『${epTitle}』パイプライン待機完了！`, 'info');
}
/**
 * ── 5. YouTube Shorts / 音楽MV制作パイプライン ──
 */
export async function runShortsBatchProduction(ctx: ProductionPipelineContext, curMode: string): Promise<void> {
  const { settings, addLog, isAbortedRef, episodesRef, setEpisodes, updateEpisode, runTasks, buildCutTasks } = ctx;
  const modeInfo = SHORTS_CONFIG_MAP[curMode] || { label: 'Shorts', unit: '本', icon: '🎬' };
  const totalEpCount = Math.max(1, settings.episodeCount || 1);
  const existingEpisodes: Episode[] = episodesRef.current || [];
  const completedEpisodes = existingEpisodes.filter(e => e.cuts.some(c => !!(c.imageBase64 || c.imageMediaId)));
  const existingMaxId = completedEpisodes.length > 0 ? Math.max(...completedEpisodes.map(e => e.id)) : 0;
  const startEpIndex = existingMaxId + 1;
  const endEpIndex = existingMaxId + totalEpCount;
  const totalCutsPerEpisode = settings.cutCount || CUTS_PER_EPISODE;
  addLog(`${modeInfo.icon} 【${modeInfo.label}モード】全 ${totalEpCount} ${modeInfo.unit}（各${totalCutsPerEpisode}Cut / 先行${settings.previewCutCount}枚描画）の制作を開始します！`, 'process');
  const baseRawTitle = settings.theme.split('（')[0].replace(/^[^\w\s一-龯]+/, '').trim() || '情景の記録';
  const defaultEnTitle = 'Cinematic Story';
  for (let epIndex = startEpIndex; epIndex <= endEpIndex; epIndex++) {
    const currentBatchStep = epIndex - startEpIndex + 1;
    if (isAbortedRef.current) {
      addLog(`🛑 制作が中断されました（${currentBatchStep - 1}/${totalEpCount}${modeInfo.unit}完了）`, 'warning');
      break;
    }
    const rawTitle = (endEpIndex > 1) ? `${baseRawTitle} Vol.${epIndex}` : baseRawTitle;
    const currentEnTitle = (endEpIndex > 1) ? `${defaultEnTitle} Vol.${epIndex}` : defaultEnTitle;
    const existingTitles = episodesRef.current
      .map((e: Episode) => e.titleJp.replace(/^[^\w\s一-龯]+/, '').trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    addLog(`${modeInfo.icon} 【第${epIndex}${modeInfo.unit}】脚本をリレー生成中（全${totalCutsPerEpisode}Cut策定）...`, 'process');
    const curPlan = {
      epNumber: epIndex,
      titleJp: rawTitle,
      titleEn: currentEnTitle,
      summary: `${baseRawTitle}の世界観で紡がれる第${epIndex}の映像作品`
    };
    const parsed = await generateSafeEpisodeScript({
      epId: epIndex,
      currentPlan: curPlan,
      country: settings.country,
      theme: settings.theme,
      era: settings.era,
      isMangaMode: false,
      isMvMode: curMode === 'mv',
      taste: settings.taste,
      productionMode: curMode,
      existingTitles,
      characterId: settings.characterId,
      targetCutCount: totalCutsPerEpisode,
      superBackoff: settings.superBackoff,
      abortCheck: () => isAbortedRef.current,
      addLog
    });
    const cutsCount = totalCutsPerEpisode;
    const baseCutsData = Array.from({ length: cutsCount }, (_, j) => {
      const cutData = parsed.cuts[j] || {} as any;
      return createPipelineCut(j, cutData, parsed, settings, curMode, curMode === 'mv', curMode === 'manga' || settings.isMangaMode);
    });
    const cleanParsedTitle = cleanEpisodeTitle(parsed.titleJp || '');
    const safeTopicJp = sanitizeForYouTubeSafety(cleanParsedTitle && cleanParsedTitle !== baseRawTitle ? cleanParsedTitle : `情景の断章`);
    const displayTitleJp = `${modeInfo.icon} ${safeTopicJp}`;
    const cleanParsedEn = cleanEpisodeTitleEn(parsed.titleEn || '');
    const displayTitleEn = cleanParsedEn && cleanParsedEn !== defaultEnTitle ? cleanParsedEn : 'Cinematic Story';
    const newEpisode: Episode = {
      id: epIndex, internalId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      titleJp: displayTitleJp, titleEn: displayTitleEn,
      summary: parsed.summary || `${safeTopicJp}の情景`, 
      characterDna: parsed.characterDna,
      eraAnalysis: parsed.eraAnalysisJp || '作品を引き立てる演出構図。',
      forbiddenAnachronisms: parsed.forbiddenAnachronisms || ['過剰な劇的演出'],
      authenticAttireEn: parsed.authenticAttireEn || 'Cinematic style attire', forbiddenKeywordsEn: 'explosive drama',
      coverCatchphraseJp: parsed.coverCatchphraseJp || '心揺さぶる一瞬の物語。', highlightWords: parsed.highlightWords || ['光'],
      cuts: baseCutsData, isGenerating: true, isGeneratingRemainingImages: false, isBatchGeneratingVideos: false,
      isPreviewDone: false, isDone: false, characterId: settings.characterId, taste: settings.taste, era: settings.era, theme: settings.theme,
      isMvMode: curMode === 'mv', productionMode: curMode as any
    };
    if (!newEpisode.isMvMode && !settings.isMvMode) {
      ctx.activeReferenceRef.current = undefined;
    }
    const turnaround = await ensureCharacterTurnaround(ctx, newEpisode);
    if (turnaround.mediaId) {
      newEpisode.characterTurnaroundMediaId = turnaround.mediaId;
      newEpisode.characterTurnaroundBase64 = turnaround.base64;
    }
    // 全カット（C01〜最終カット）を即座にUIへ反映して横並びSTANDBY展開
    setEpisodes((prev: Episode[]) => [...prev.filter(e => e.id !== epIndex), newEpisode]);
    episodesRef.current = [...episodesRef.current.filter((e: Episode) => e.id !== epIndex), newEpisode];
    addLog(`✨ 第${epIndex}${modeInfo.unit}『${newEpisode.titleJp}』全${cutsCount}カットの脚本確定！（C01〜C${String(cutsCount).padStart(2, '0')}を配置、先行${settings.previewCutCount}枚を描画開始）`, 'success');
    const targetCutCount = Math.min(settings.previewCutCount, cutsCount);
    await runTasks(buildCutTasks(newEpisode, newEpisode.cuts.slice(0, targetCutCount)));
    if (isAbortedRef.current) {
      updateEpisode(epIndex, { isGenerating: false });
      break;
    }
    const freshNewEp = episodesRef.current.find((e: Episode) => e.id === epIndex) || newEpisode;
    const completedCount = freshNewEp.cuts.slice(0, targetCutCount).filter(c => c.imageBase64 || c.imageMediaId).length;
    const isAllDone = completedCount >= cutsCount;
    const isPreviewSuccessful = completedCount >= targetCutCount;
    updateEpisode(epIndex, { 
      isGenerating: false, 
      isPreviewDone: isPreviewSuccessful, 
      isDone: isAllDone 
    });
    if (isPreviewSuccessful) {
      addLog(`✅ 第${epIndex}${modeInfo.unit}『${newEpisode.titleJp}』先行${targetCutCount}カットの画像生成が完了しました！（残り${cutsCount - targetCutCount}カットはSTANDBY待機）`, 'success');
      await ensureInfographicCover(ctx, freshNewEp);
    }
    await finalizeAndProcessEpisode(ctx, epIndex, {
      unitLabel: modeInfo.unit,
      title: newEpisode.titleJp
    });
  }
  if (!isAbortedRef.current) {
    addLog(`🎉 全 ${totalEpCount} ${modeInfo.unit}の${modeInfo.label}先行制作が完了しました！`, 'success');
  }
}
/**
 * ── 6. 連続ドラマ・漫画シリーズ制作パイプライン ──
 */
export async function runSeriesProduction(ctx: ProductionPipelineContext): Promise<void> {
  const { 
    settings, 
    addLog, 
    isAbortedRef, 
    episodesRef, 
    setEpisodes, 
    updateEpisode, 
    runTasks, 
    buildCutTasks, 
    seriesManifestRef, 
    setActiveSeriesManifest, 
    currentAssetRef, 
    activeReferenceRef 
  } = ctx;
  const fixedChar = (settings.characterId && settings.characterId !== 'auto')
    ? SAFE_CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === settings.characterId)
    : undefined;
  const totalCutsPerEpisode = settings.cutCount || CUTS_PER_EPISODE;
  if (!seriesManifestRef.current) {
    const isHist = checkIsHistorical(settings.era, settings.theme);
    const isMv = settings.isMvMode || settings.productionMode === 'mv';
    const isManga = settings.isMangaMode || settings.productionMode === 'manga';
    const genreLabel = isMv ? '音楽MVシリーズ' : isManga ? '漫画シリーズ' : isHist ? '大河ドラマ' : '連続ドラマ';
    const totalEps = settings.totalEpisodes || settings.episodeCount || 1;
    addLog(`📜 全${totalEps}話の${genreLabel}グランドデザインをAIに策定依頼中... [世界観: ${settings.theme}]`, 'process');
    const EP_CHUNK_SIZE = 20;
    const epChunks = Math.ceil(totalEps / EP_CHUNK_SIZE);
    let allEpisodesPlan: SeriesEpisodePlan[] = [];
    let seriesTitle = settings.theme.split('（')[0].replace(/^[^\w\s一-龯]+/, '').trim() || '物語の記録';
    let overallSynopsis = `${settings.theme}の世界観で描かれる物語`;
    for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < epChunks; cIdx++) {
      if (isAbortedRef.current) break;
      const startEp = cIdx * EP_CHUNK_SIZE + 1;
      const endEp = Math.min((cIdx + 1) * EP_CHUNK_SIZE, totalEps);
      const prevHeaders = allEpisodesPlan.length > 0
        ? allEpisodesPlan.map(p => `[第${p.epNumber}話] ${p.titleJp}: ${p.summary}`).join('\n')
        : '';
      const designPrompt = buildGrandDesignChunkPrompt(
        startEp,
        endEp,
        totalEps,
        settings.country,
        settings.theme,
        settings.era,
        isManga,
        isMv,
        fixedChar?.dnaPromptEn,
        fixedChar?.nameJa,
        prevHeaders
      );
      let designRes: any;
      try {
        designRes = await callWithRetry<any>(() => Flow.generate.text(designPrompt), undefined, 2);
      } catch (_) {
        designRes = { text: '' };
      }
      const parsedDesign = safeJsonParse<any>(designRes?.text || '', {});
      if (cIdx === 0 && parsedDesign.seriesTitle) seriesTitle = parsedDesign.seriesTitle;
      if (cIdx === 0 && parsedDesign.overallSynopsis) overallSynopsis = parsedDesign.overallSynopsis;
      const rawPlanList: SeriesEpisodePlan[] = Array.isArray(parsedDesign.episodesPlan) ? parsedDesign.episodesPlan : [];
      for (let eNum = startEp; eNum <= endEp; eNum++) {
        const found = rawPlanList.find((p: any) => p.epNumber === eNum);
        allEpisodesPlan.push({
          epNumber: eNum,
          titleJp: found?.titleJp ? cleanEpisodeTitle(found.titleJp) : (totalEps > 1 ? `${seriesTitle} 第${eNum}章` : seriesTitle),
          titleEn: found?.titleEn ? cleanEpisodeTitleEn(found.titleEn) : `Episode ${eNum}`,
          summary: found?.summary || `${settings.theme}の世界観で描かれる物語`
        });
      }
    }
    seriesManifestRef.current = {
      seriesTitle,
      totalEpisodes: totalEps,
      currentEpisodeId: 1,
      completedEpisodeIds: [],
      overallSynopsis,
      episodesPlan: allEpisodesPlan,
      referenceAsset: currentAssetRef.current ? {
        ...currentAssetRef.current,
        characterDna: activeReferenceRef.current?.characterDna,
        styleDna: activeReferenceRef.current?.styleDna,
        antiPoseNegative: activeReferenceRef.current?.antiPoseNegative,
